## GlotLID v4

GlotLID v4 (`model_v4.bin`, also `model.bin`) supports **2060 labels** (1,900 ISO 639-3 language codes with script, plus the "zxx" and "und" series). GlotLID v4 small (`model_v4_small.bin`) has the same labels. Both are trained with [fastText-turbo](https://github.com/CommonGlot/fastText-turbo) on the [GlotLID corpus](https://huggingface.co/datasets/cis-lmu/glotlid-corpus).

**Changes from v3**

v3 has 2102 labels and v4 has 2060: 179 v3 labels are gone and 137 are new. In v4, a label is kept only if it works on independent benchmarks; otherwise its data is merged into the label it cannot be told apart from, or the whole label is moved to the corpus's `excluded/` folder.

- **Quechua.** The 27 Quechua variety labels of v3 (`qub_Latn`, `quf_Latn`, `qug_Latn`, `quh_Latn`, `qul_Latn`, `qup_Latn`, `qus_Latn`, `quw_Latn`, `quy_Latn`, `quz_Latn`, `qva_Latn`, `qvc_Latn`, `qve_Latn`, `qvh_Latn`, `qvi_Latn`, `qvm_Latn`, `qvn_Latn`, `qvo_Latn`, `qvs_Latn`, `qvw_Latn`, `qvz_Latn`, `qwh_Latn`, `qxh_Latn`, `qxl_Latn`, `qxn_Latn`, `qxo_Latn`, `qxr_Latn`) are merged into `que_Latn`.
- **Uzbek and Tagalog.** Uzbek uses the macrolanguage code: `uzn_Latn`, `uzn_Cyrl`, `uzs_Arab` are now `uzb_Latn`, `uzb_Cyrl`, `uzb_Arab`. `fil_Latn` is now `tgl_Latn`.
- **Merged into a close label** (the corpus keeps the data as `LABEL_source+code.txt`): `bcc_Arab` → `bal_Arab`, `fkv_Latn` → `fit_Latn`, `lrc_Arab` → `bqi_Arab`, `pua_Latn` → `tsz_Latn`, `sdc_Latn` → `srd_Latn`, `sjo_Mong` → `und_Mong`, `syc_Syrc` → `aii_Syrc`, `tdt_Latn` → `tet_Latn`, `thv_Latn` → `taq_Latn`, `tpw_Latn` → `tpn_Latn`, `tzm_Tfng` → `zgh_Tfng`.
- **Excluded** (not reliable on independent benchmarks; the data is in the corpus's `excluded/` folder): `acm_Arab`, `apc_Arab` (with `ajp_Arab`, which ISO 639-3 merged into `apc` in 2023), `ars_Arab`, `awa_Deva`, `ayp_Arab`, `bew_Latn`, `cnr_Latn`, `csw_Cans`, `kex_Deva`, `nan_Hani`, `shu_Arab`.
- **Also removed:** `ckm_Latn`, `kdp_Latn`, `nrm_Latn`.
- **`und` labels.** v3 had 157 `und_` labels, one per writing system, made of random characters of the script; 37 of them were for scripts that also had language labels. v4 keeps an `und_` label only for scripts without any language label: where the corpus now has text in a script, v4 has the language instead (102 new labels such as `egy_Egyp`, `txg_Tang`, `iii_Yiii`, `xlc_Lyci`), and `und_` is dropped for scripts that already had languages (e.g. `und_Beng`, `und_Cyrl`, `und_Hira`). v4 adds `und_` labels for 7 newly encoded scripts (`und_Gara`, `und_Gukh`, `und_Onao`, `und_Sidt`, `und_Tayo`, `und_Tols`, `und_Tutg`).
- **New non-linguistic labels:** `zxx_Code` (source code), `zxx_Zmth` (mathematical notation) and `zxx_Zsym` (symbols), next to v3's `zxx_Latn`, `zxx_Arab` and `zxx_Zzzz`.
- **New languages and scripts** (21): `aar_Latn`, `akk_Latn`, `bal_Arab`, `bap_Krai`, `bqi_Arab`, `cdo_Latn`, `egy_Latn`, `grt_Latn`, `izh_Latn`, `kpo_Latn`, `otk_Latn`, `ram_Latn`, `rif_Latn`, `rmf_Latn`, `snk_Latn`, `sqi_Todr`, `srb_Latn`, `sux_Latn`, `suz_Sunu`, `tly_Arab`, `zag_Berf`.

**Training on short and noisy text**

During training, each line is cut to a random span of 5 to 40 characters with probability 0.15 and normalized (lowercased, without punctuation and digits) with probability 0.15, which makes v4 much better on short inputs and its probabilities better calibrated.

Parameters: v4 is a softmax model with dim 96, 2 epochs, lr 0.8, character n-grams 2 to 5, wordNgrams 1, 2M buckets and minCount 20000; v4 small uses hierarchical softmax with dim 16 and 10 epochs, otherwise the same. The training data is the GlotLID corpus upsampled with alpha 0.3.

### Supported labels and performance

The scores below are on the held-out test split of v4's training data (up to 1,000 lines per label, from the same sources as the training data, so they are higher than on independent benchmarks). "total number of sentences" is the number of lines of the label in the corpus, before upsampling; "mean probability of true predictions" is the mean top probability of the correctly predicted lines.
